# Commentary v7 scope prose

Continue as the **micro** scope agent for **29:38**. Read the complete
discovery below and develop fluent Turkish scope prose. Discover further
readings within your lane as you explain the evidence.

## Reading Purpose

The purpose is to reveal secondary readings that expand, complicate, or shift
how the passage is read. Keep the ordinary sense available as orientation, then
develop the additional readings. A general restatement of the main meaning does
not accomplish this task.

Actively discover at every stage, including composition, consolidation, and
editorial. Earlier findings are a starting point, not a ceiling. Investigate
plausible contacts beyond the candidates and revisit earlier exclusions when
the supplied evidence permits a reading. Do not adopt a conservative preference
for familiar interpretations. Unconventionality, uncertainty, source-status
labels, or absence from an earlier finding list are not reasons to suppress a
possibility. Develop materially distinct readings together; qualify uncertainty
where it occurs rather than retreating to a generalized main reading.

Make every reading's reasoning visible to someone who does not know Arabic:
show the particular wording, construction, attested sense, image, or contextual
passage; explain its connection to the focus; and state what that connection
changes in the interpretation. Distinguish the source fact from your inference.
A conclusion or evocative image alone does not preserve a finding. Include a
brief boundary where misunderstanding is likely; avoid repetitive disclaimers.
The reader should be able to understand, remember, and challenge the connection.

Coherence comes from arranging explicit readings and explaining their
relationships. Compatible readings may share a paragraph only while each
reading's evidence, connection, and interpretive consequence remain visible.
Remove duplicated wording, not distinct meanings or their supporting links.
Two readings are duplicates only when their evidence, connection, and
interpretive consequence are equivalent. A shared conclusion is insufficient.
Keep ordinary glosses separate from inferred scenes; explain the additional
reading in the surrounding prose. Preserve the name of the attested lexical
item and its link to the passage, not just an evocative source-image description.


## Working With The Inputs

The discovery is included in full. The appended source records are copied by
the workflow with their original wording, qualifications, and morphology.
They let you check the evidence without reconstructing an earlier agent's
summary. Initial findings and exclusions are revisable judgments, not a ceiling.
Preserve each supported mechanism; correct factual mistakes from the evidence.
If counterevidence defeats a reading, explain the correction briefly in your
completion reply. Do not silently drop it or fabricate support.

Use the supplied reader, not custom scripts, parsers, projections, or generators.
Read the complete discovery with:

```bash
python3 _commentary/v7/authoring.py read --prompt _commentary/v7/input/pilot-29-38-p03-fatiha-r2-202609070227/s029/29_38/micro.composition.prompt.md --block authoring_inputs
```

Continue at `next_offset` with the same `--block` to read the remaining text.
Read the source appendix as needed for verification, not as a mandatory second
survey. A read without selectors returns instructions and a block index.
Use one read per tool call with at least 16,000 output tokens; recover failed
or truncated reads.
Additional evidence remains available from the same sealed packet:

- micro: `python3 _commentary/v7/authoring.py read --prompt _commentary/v7/input/pilot-29-38-p03-fatiha-r2-202609070227/s029/29_38/micro.discovery.prompt.md --section SECTION`

Use `--refs support:ID branch:ROOT/BRANCH context:S:A` for whole records. Source
paths inside those records are provenance. Current-stage instructions govern;
discovery instructions around original data blocks do not reopen your role.

## Writing

Explain each distinct evidence-to-interpretation chain. Several mechanisms may
share a paragraph while retaining their individual wording, connection, and
consequence. Keep lexical items, concrete images, form restrictions, and every
contributing ayah's particular role visible. Boundaries stay short and local.

Use `{ar:ARABIC, tr:transliteration, gloss:Turkish gloss}` at each Arabic anchor,
repeating it when the item does interpretive work in another paragraph. The
gloss describes the actual word or phrase; inference belongs outside the tag.
Cite each non-focus ayah beside its own contribution, using individual refs,
not intervals. Check quotations against the named source rather than moving a
nearby word into that ayah. Internal IDs and lane machinery stay out of prose.

Write only `_commentary/v7/raw/pilot-29-38-p03-fatiha-r2-202609070227/s029/29_38/micro.scope.tr.md`, using the file editor. Preserve the discovery
JSON as the initial snapshot; new findings belong in the prose. Before finishing,
compare the explanations, not just their headings or references: each input
mechanism must still be explicit. Run any supplied terminal lifecycle command.

      "evidence_refs": ["support:sup_2bea6964aaa743a336e7", "support:sup_5554e8dd7170847aeef2", "support:sup_2dc9308e4a48584e10df", "support:sup_bcdc904d2f4e99fc5789", "support:sup_817cfe53987a7f759c2b", "support:sup_fd46f44f3ce5c2eb14b8", "support:sup_82c011242f54b6d2ac3e", "branch:root_000121/B002"]
    },
    {
      "finding_ref": "micro:body-insight-contrast",
      "title": "Bodily collapse is set against qualified insight",
      "reading": "The supplied boundary evidence places the closing participle beside an earlier bodily-collapse image, creating a body-versus-mind contrast: visible ruin and fallen bodies do not imply absent perception. Form X in `مُسْتَبْصِرِينَ` heightens the field of discernment, but the evidence also allows seeking, possessing, or claiming insight, so the reading must not force maximal certainty. What changes is the failure diagnosis: the scene can hold cognitive capacity or a claim to insight together with conduct that still does not follow the recognized path.",
      "evidence_refs": ["support:sup_8118c110735dee93b86c", "support:sup_4d642aa41188c2f2d3f2", "support:sup_82c011242f54b6d2ac3e", "branch:root_000121/B002"]
    },
    {
      "finding_ref": "micro:insight-closure",
      "title": "The ending closes a clarity-available failure envelope",
      "reading": "The closing insight participle is linked by its row family to the opening `تَبَيَّنَ` clarity wording and to the concessive final clause. The evidence has already been certified as manifest to the audience, and the peoples are then described in a heightened perceptual state; the envelope closes precisely where perception fails to govern action. This does not claim that every shade of the root is active at once. It preserves the specific connection between established external disclosure, qualified inner insight, and the completed diversion.",
      "evidence_refs": ["support:sup_d103e3976ebe2743182d", "support:sup_82c011242f54b6d2ac3e", "support:sup_c00c0ef0e0a8e5837571", "support:sup_2bea6964aaa743a336e7"]
    },
    {
      "finding_ref": "micro:proper-name-form",
      "title": "The paired names keep different case surfaces",
      "reading": "`عَادًا` carries accusative tanwin and is treated as a carried object under a recovered governing frame. Its pairing with `ثَمُودَ` is syntactically shared, but the base form of Thamud remains diptotic and therefore does not take the same tanwin. The supplied leveling variant shows that a different surface was possible, making the received asymmetry a live formal pressure rather than a necessary change in objecthood. Case thus keeps the compact name-pair together while allowing the two names to sound different.",
      "evidence_refs": ["support:sup_c4f5224f8d7df57e56d2", "support:sup_df0aa5d3a60869d9e658", "support:sup_f969f73aeab0fad1034d", "support:sup_f4525684e125d6460f6b"]
    },
    {
      "finding_ref": "micro:proper-name-root-pressure",
      "title": "A return-root echo colors the ʿAd name cautiously",
      "reading": "The local item `عَادًا` remains a proper name, so the `ع و د` field of return and recurrence cannot replace its referential function with a free verb meaning. It can, however, create a restrained irony: a name carrying return pressure appears in a ruin scene marked by failed return to the recognized way. The connection is background coloring only, with the name's case and paired-object role kept primary.",
      "evidence_refs": ["support:sup_8b2964925237c64a4e53", "support:sup_df0aa5d3a60869d9e658"]
    },
    {
      "finding_ref": "micro:th-name-pressure",
      "title": "The Thamud name carries a faint depletion image",
      "reading": "`ثَمُودَ` is locally a proper name in the same carried accusative sequence. The supplied root field permits a cautious exhausted-water or depletion image beside the visible remains, but the evidence explicitly narrows that pressure to background coloring. The reading therefore adds a resource-exhaustion echo to the ruin scene without turning the name into an ordinary lexical noun or displacing its proper-name role.",
      "evidence_refs": ["support:sup_80e29439989f89b33a51", "support:sup_f4525684e125d6460f6b"]
    },
    {
      "finding_ref": "micro:baseline-evidence-gap",
      "title": "Available evidence and insight still fail to govern movement",
      "reading": "The qualified focus-local synthesis assembles `تَبَيَّنَ` B004 as emergence and disclosure, `مَسَٰكِنِهِمْ` B002 as settled sites, and `مُسْتَبْصِرِينَ` B002 as inward discernment. Their connection is specific: outward evidence is available from the remains and inner perception is also described, yet the peoples are still diverted. The interpretive change is from a simple failure to see toward a failure of discernment to govern conduct. The nomination is legacy-unbound, so this is a qualified assembled mechanism rather than an independent wider-context proof.",
      "evidence_refs": ["support:sup_ef7feaea18c97da4d0c0", "branch:root_000170/B004", "branch:root_000726/B002", "branch:root_000121/B002"]
    },
    {
      "finding_ref": "micro:baseline-valuation-capture",
      "title": "Attractive revaluation reroutes active agency",
      "reading": "The qualified valuation model links beautifying display (zyn B002), the overstepping adversarial agent (shṭn B004), intentional work (ʿml B001), turning away (ṣdd B001), and the traversed way (sbl B001). The sequence matters: their own purposive deeds remain active, but attractive presentation captures their evaluation of those deeds and reroutes agency toward diversion. This focus-local reading changes the image from an external tempter simply hiding an unseen road to an action-loop in which valuation mediates movement. The source's legacy status is kept visible, and the lexical branches are treated as qualified contacts rather than a forced single root meaning.",
      "evidence_refs": ["support:sup_850fd7762f8a652a0825", "branch:root_000660/B002", "branch:root_000796/B004", "branch:root_001046/B001", "branch:root_000848/B001", "branch:root_000672/B001"]
    },
    {
      "finding_ref": "micro:baseline-settlement",
      "title": "The settled place reverses into testimony",
      "reading": "The settlement-reversal nomination connects `س ك ن` B001, the cessation of motion, with B009, a settled place, and `ب ي ن` B004, becoming manifest. A dwelling normally stabilizes life by giving it a place to remain; here the settled sites outlast their inhabitants only as visible evidence against their claim to durable stability. This mechanism is distinct from the broader evidence-insight gap because it focuses on the reversal of settlement itself. It remains a qualified focus-local model supplied with legacy provenance.",
      "evidence_refs": ["support:sup_15b705015e215d3c1e0d", "branch:root_000726/B001", "branch:root_000726/B009", "branch:root_000170/B004"]
    },
    {
      "finding_ref": "micro:published-barrier",
      "title": "Adorned works place a barrier between evidence and way",
      "reading": "The supplied published finding joins four concrete contacts: the dwellings become visible evidence through `ب ي ن` B004, inner discernment remains available through `ب ص ر` B002, beautification alters the presentation of deeds through `ز ي ن` B002, and turning away can be heard as a blocking barrier through `ص د د` B001/B005. The resulting reading is that evaluative adornment occupies the space between what can be known and the path that should be followed. The source is a citable cross-run synthesis, but the explanation stays within the assembled focus-local mechanisms and does not import an unstated wider scene.",
      "evidence_refs": ["support:sup_3e1351fa35815a1bbc9f", "branch:root_000121/B002", "branch:root_000170/B004", "branch:root_000660/B002", "branch:root_000848/B001", "branch:root_000848/B005"]
    },

      "title": "Decorated sight can become a blockage",
      "reading": "The focus-local reader activation assembles `ص د د` B001, turning aside, with B005, a blocking mountain image. The dwellings make the outcome visible, while beautified deeds are inferred to form an intervening obstruction between perception and path; the closing perceptive state then intensifies the warning because capacity to see can coexist with a constructed blockage. The packet directly supplies the lexical sequence and the obstruction frame; the mental redirection is an inference and is therefore kept explicitly qualified.",
      "evidence_refs": ["support:sup_a77d2794b80076f119e6", "branch:root_000848/B001", "branch:root_000848/B005"]
    },

      "title": "Adorned work captures valuation before the turn",
      "reading": "The second focus-local reader activation links `زَيَّنَ` B002, `ٱلشَّيْطَانُ` through the supplied opposition/crookedness branch B003, `أَعْمَالَهُمْ` B001, `فَصَدَّهُمْ` B001, and `ٱلسَّبِيلِ` B001. The explicit fa places beautification before diversion, while the retained insight state keeps the mechanism centered on valuation rather than missing information. Assigned role: adornment alters the appearance of their own work; crooked opposition exploits that evaluation to redirect movement. {"evidence_ref":"support:sup_0b0d79691242755a9e0c","record":{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"context_refs":["6:43","8:48","15:39","16:63","27:24"],"quran_refs":["6:43","8:48","15:39","16:63","27:24","29:38"],"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"micro","source_local_id":"29:38:15","source_type":"word_analysis","support_id":"sup_0b0d79691242755a9e0c","text":"{\"gloss_range\":\"definite nominative adversarial agent who beautifies the deeds; remoteness pressure fits the later separation from the path\",\"prose\":\"{{ar:ٱلشَّيْطَانُ}} ({{tr:al-shayṭānu}}) is definite and nominative, so the beautifying agent is a known adversary, not an unnamed force. {"evidence_ref":"support:sup_1e2a7b6dd69b3815b31c","record":{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"context_refs":["29:35"],"quran_refs":["29:35","29:38"],"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"micro","source_local_id":"29:38:7","source_type":"word_analysis","support_id":"sup_1e2a7b6dd69b3815b31c","text":"{\"gloss_range\":\"Form V perfect of becoming clear or distinct; the evidence has self-manifested to the audience from the ruins\",\"prose\":\"{{ar:تَبَيَّنَ}} ({{tr:tabayyana}}) presents the ruin evidence as something that has already become clear to the audience. {"evidence_ref":"support:sup_8ddab411dc46fedd646f","record":{"branch_refs":["root_000660/B002","root_000672/B001","root_000796/B003","root_000848/B001","root_001046/B001"],"citable":false,"context_refs":[],"quran_refs":[],"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"micro","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_8ddab411dc46fedd646f","text":"2. **Adorned work captures evaluation and turns agents from the traversed way.** Lexical evidence: `زَيَّن`, root `ز ي ن`, `B002` (making beauty show); `ٱلشَّيْطَان`, root `ش ط ن`, `B003` (opposition, crookedness, and harsh difficulty); `أَعْمَالَهُم`, root `ع م ل`, `B001` (intentional work); `فَصَدَّهُم`, root `ص د د`, `B001` (turning aside); and `ٱلسَّبِيل`, root `س ب ل`, `B001` (extended traversed way). Structural cue: beautification precedes diversion by explicit `فَ`, despite retained inner sight. Assigned role: adornment alters the appearance of their own work; crooked opposition exploits that evaluation to redirect movement. Abductive move: the packet supplies sequence but not the mental arrow, so I infer that attractive self-appraisal mediates the turn. Reading change: misguidance operates through captured valuation of one's deeds, not lack of evidence.","trust":"legacy_unbound","upstream_scope":"global"}},
